Package: BGNLMforSINDy
Type: Package
Title: Bayesian Generalized Nonlinear Models for Basis-Free SINDy with Model Uncertainty
Version: 1.0.0
Authors@R: c(
    person("Aliaksandr", "Hubin", email = "aliaksandr.hubin@nmbu.no", role = c("aut", "cre")),
    person("Jon", "Lachmann", role = "aut")
  )
Description: Reproducible experimental benchmark pipeline for ICML expanding
    "Bayesian Generalized Nonlinear Models Offer Basis Free SINDy With Model Uncertainty".
    Evaluates basis-free BGNLM (GMJMCMC) against predefined-basis BGLM (MJMCMC),
    classical and Bayesian/Ensemble SINDy, SINDy-PI, and Symbolic Regression (PySR, PyOperon)
    across 12 synthetic ODE systems and 5 real-world empirical datasets with mixed-effects
    statistical evaluation.

.PHONY: all install data manifests test smoke tune synth real analyze clean

R ?= Rscript
WORKERS ?= 4
THREADS ?= 2

all: install data manifests test smoke

install:
	$(R) scripts/install_dependencies.R

data:
	$(R) scripts/download_real_data.R

manifests:
	$(R) scripts/generate_manifest.R

test:
	$(R) -e 'testthat::test_dir("tests/testthat", stop_on_failure = TRUE)'

tune:
	$(R) scripts/run_tuning.R

smoke:
	$(R) scripts/generate_manifest.R --smoke_test
	bash scripts/run_remote_server.sh both $(WORKERS) $(THREADS)

synth:
	bash scripts/run_remote_server.sh synth $(WORKERS) $(THREADS)

real:
	bash scripts/run_remote_server.sh real $(WORKERS) $(THREADS)

analyze:
	$(R) scripts/aggregate_results.R
	$(R) scripts/run_mixed_models.R

clean:
	rm -rf results/checkpoints_synth results/checkpoints_real logs

FILE: R/00_env_setup.R

# ==============================================================================
# R/00_env_setup.R
# Enforces Project-Local R Library (.r_lib) & Python Virtualenv (.venv)
# Guarantees deterministic, self-contained execution on shared Linux/HPC servers
# ==============================================================================

setup_project_env <- function() {
  # Locate repository root (works from repo root or tests/testthat)
  candidates <- c(".", "..", "../..")
  repo_root <- "."
  for (cand in candidates) {
    if (file.exists(file.path(cand, "DESCRIPTION")) && dir.exists(file.path(cand, "config"))) {
      repo_root <- normalizePath(cand, mustWork = TRUE)
      break
    }
  }

  local_r_lib <- file.path(repo_root, ".r_lib")
  if (dir.exists(local_r_lib)) {
    local_r_lib <- normalizePath(local_r_lib, mustWork = TRUE)
    # Place project-local .r_lib strictly first, followed by base .Library and existing paths
    .libPaths(unique(c(local_r_lib, .Library, .libPaths())))
    Sys.setenv(R_LIBS_USER = local_r_lib, R_LIBS = local_r_lib)
  }

  venv_py <- file.path(repo_root, ".venv", "bin", "python")
  if (file.exists(venv_py)) {
    venv_py_dir <- normalizePath(dirname(venv_py), mustWork = TRUE)
    venv_py_abs <- file.path(venv_py_dir, "python")
    Sys.setenv(RETICULATE_PYTHON = venv_py_abs)
    cur_path <- Sys.getenv("PATH")
    if (!startsWith(cur_path, venv_py_dir)) {
      Sys.setenv(PATH = paste(venv_py_dir, cur_path, sep = .Platform$path.sep))
    }
  }

  invisible(list(repo_root = repo_root, local_r_lib = local_r_lib))
}

setup_project_env()

# ==============================================================================
# R/01_ode_simulators.R
# 12 Canonical SINDy Benchmark Dynamical Systems, Noise Regimes, & Differentiation
# ==============================================================================

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(deSolve)
  library(signal)
})

#' Return complete Registry of the 12 Benchmark Dynamical Systems
get_systems_registry <- function() {
  list(
    sys01_linear_quadratic_3d = list(
      sys_id = 1L,
      id = "sys01_linear_quadratic_3d",
      name = "Linear-Quadratic Coupled 3D",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 3L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 2.0, x2 = 0.0, x3 = 1.0),
      parameters = c(a = -1.0, b = 20.0, c = -20.0, d = -1.0, e = -3.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 20.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]; x3 <- state[3]
        dx1 <- parms["a"] * x1 + parms["b"] * x1 * x2
        dx2 <- parms["c"] * x1 + parms["d"] * x1 * x2
        dx3 <- parms["e"] * x3
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]; x3 <- X[, 3]
        cbind(
          dx1 = parms["a"] * x1 + parms["b"] * x1 * x2,
          dx2 = parms["c"] * x1 + parms["d"] * x1 * x2,
          dx3 = parms["e"] * x3
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "x1*x2"),
        c("x1", "x1*x2"),
        c("x3")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = -1.0, "x1*x2" = 20.0),
        c("x1" = -20.0, "x1*x2" = -1.0),
        c("x3" = -3.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys02_lorenz63 = list(
      sys_id = 2L,
      id = "sys02_lorenz63",
      name = "Lorenz-63 System",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 3L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = -0.5, x2 = -2.0, x3 = 3.0),
      parameters = c(sigma = 10.0, rho = 28.0, beta = 8.0 / 3.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 50.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]; x3 <- state[3]
        dx1 <- parms["sigma"] * (x2 - x1)
        dx2 <- x1 * (parms["rho"] - x3) - x2
        dx3 <- x1 * x2 - parms["beta"] * x3
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]; x3 <- X[, 3]
        cbind(
          dx1 = parms["sigma"] * (x2 - x1),
          dx2 = x1 * (parms["rho"] - x3) - x2,
          dx3 = x1 * x2 - parms["beta"] * x3
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "x2"),
        c("x1", "x2", "x1*x3"),
        c("x3", "x1*x2")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = -10.0, "x2" = 10.0),
        c("x1" = 28.0, "x2" = -1.0, "x1*x3" = -1.0),
        c("x3" = -8.0 / 3.0, "x1*x2" = 1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys03_rossler = list(
      sys_id = 3L,
      id = "sys03_rossler",
      name = "Rossler Attractor",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 3L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 0.5, x2 = -1.0, x3 = 2.0),
      parameters = c(a = 0.2, b = 0.2, c = 5.7),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 50.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]; x3 <- state[3]
        dx1 <- -x2 - x3
        dx2 <- x1 + parms["a"] * x2
        dx3 <- parms["b"] + x3 * (x1 - parms["c"])
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]; x3 <- X[, 3]
        cbind(
          dx1 = -x2 - x3,
          dx2 = x1 + parms["a"] * x2,
          dx3 = parms["b"] + x3 * (x1 - parms["c"])
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x2", "x3"),
        c("x1", "x2"),
        c("x3", "x1*x3")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x2" = -1.0, "x3" = -1.0),
        c("x1" = 1.0, "x2" = 0.2),
        c("x3" = -5.7, "x1*x3" = 1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys04_lotka_volterra = list(
      sys_id = 4L,
      id = "sys04_lotka_volterra",
      name = "Lotka-Volterra Predator-Prey",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 2L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 1.0, x2 = 1.0),
      parameters = c(alpha = 1.5, beta = 1.0, delta = 1.0, gamma = 3.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 30.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]
        dx1 <- parms["alpha"] * x1 - parms["beta"] * x1 * x2
        dx2 <- parms["delta"] * x1 * x2 - parms["gamma"] * x2
        list(c(dx1, dx2))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]
        cbind(
          dx1 = parms["alpha"] * x1 - parms["beta"] * x1 * x2,
          dx2 = parms["delta"] * x1 * x2 - parms["gamma"] * x2
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "x1*x2"),
        c("x2", "x1*x2")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = 1.5, "x1*x2" = -1.0),
        c("x2" = -3.0, "x1*x2" = 1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys05_van_der_pol = list(
      sys_id = 5L,
      id = "sys05_van_der_pol",
      name = "Van der Pol Oscillator",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 2L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 2.0, x2 = 0.0),
      parameters = c(mu = 2.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 30.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]
        dx1 <- x2
        dx2 <- parms["mu"] * (1.0 - x1^2) * x2 - x1
        list(c(dx1, dx2))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]
        cbind(
          dx1 = x2,
          dx2 = parms["mu"] * (1.0 - x1^2) * x2 - x1
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x2"),
        c("x1", "x2", "x1^2*x2")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x2" = 1.0),
        c("x1" = -1.0, "x2" = 2.0, "x1^2*x2" = -2.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys06_duffing = list(
      sys_id = 6L,
      id = "sys06_duffing",
      name = "Duffing Oscillator",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 2L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 0.5, x2 = 1.0),
      parameters = c(delta = 0.1, alpha = -1.0, beta = 1.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 30.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]
        dx1 <- x2
        dx2 <- -parms["delta"] * x2 - parms["alpha"] * x1 - parms["beta"] * x1^3
        list(c(dx1, dx2))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]
        cbind(
          dx1 = x2,
          dx2 = -parms["delta"] * x2 - parms["alpha"] * x1 - parms["beta"] * x1^3
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x2"),
        c("x1", "x2", "x1^3")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x2" = 1.0),
        c("x1" = 1.0, "x2" = -0.1, "x1^3" = -1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys07_brusselator = list(
      sys_id = 7L,
      id = "sys07_brusselator",
      name = "Brusselator System",
      group = "Group_A_Polynomial",
      dim = 2L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 1.5, x2 = 3.0),
      parameters = c(a = 1.0, b = 3.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 30.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]
        dx1 <- parms["a"] - (parms["b"] + 1.0) * x1 + (x1^2) * x2
        dx2 <- parms["b"] * x1 - (x1^2) * x2
        list(c(dx1, dx2))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]
        cbind(
          dx1 = parms["a"] - (parms["b"] + 1.0) * x1 + (x1^2) * x2,
          dx2 = parms["b"] * x1 - (x1^2) * x2
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "x1^2*x2"),
        c("x1", "x1^2*x2")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = -4.0, "x1^2*x2" = 1.0),
        c("x1" = 3.0, "x1^2*x2" = -1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),

    sys08_rossler_lorenz_hybrid = list(
      sys_id = 8L,
      id = "sys08_rossler_lorenz_hybrid",
      name = "Rossler-Lorenz Hybrid 3D",
      group = "Group_B_NonPolynomial",
      dim = 3L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 0.5, x2 = -1.0, x3 = 2.0),
      parameters = c(a = 0.2, b = 0.1, c = 0.4, d = 5.7),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 50.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]; x3 <- state[3]
        dx1 <- -x2 - x3 + parms["a"] * sin(x1)
        dx2 <- x1 + parms["b"] * x2
        dx3 <- parms["c"] + x3 * (x1 - parms["d"])
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]; x3 <- X[, 3]
        cbind(
          dx1 = -x2 - x3 + parms["a"] * sin(x1),
          dx2 = x1 + parms["b"] * x2,
          dx3 = parms["c"] + x3 * (x1 - parms["d"])
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x2", "x3", "sin(x1)"),
        c("x1", "x2"),
        c("x3", "x1*x3")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x2" = -1.0, "x3" = -1.0, "sin(x1)" = 0.2),
        c("x1" = 1.0, "x2" = 0.1),
        c("x3" = -5.7, "x1*x3" = 1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = c("sin(x1)", "cos(x1)")
    ),

    sys09_damped_pendulum = list(
      sys_id = 9L,
      id = "sys09_damped_pendulum",
      name = "Damped Nonlinear Pendulum",
      group = "Group_B_NonPolynomial",
      dim = 2L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = pi - 0.1, x2 = 0.0),
      parameters = c(gamma = 0.25, g_over_L = 5.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 25.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]
        dx1 <- x2
        dx2 <- -parms["gamma"] * x2 - parms["g_over_L"] * sin(x1)
        list(c(dx1, dx2))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]
        cbind(
          dx1 = x2,
          dx2 = -parms["gamma"] * x2 - parms["g_over_L"] * sin(x1)
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x2"),
        c("x2", "sin(x1)")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x2" = 1.0),
        c("x2" = -0.25, "sin(x1)" = -5.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = c("sin(x1)", "cos(x1)")
    ),

    sys10_thomas_attractor = list(
      sys_id = 10L,
      id = "sys10_thomas_attractor",
      name = "Thomas Cyclically Symmetric Attractor",
      group = "Group_B_NonPolynomial",
      dim = 3L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 0.1, x2 = 0.0, x3 = 0.0),
      parameters = c(b = 0.18),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 50.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]; x3 <- state[3]
        dx1 <- sin(x2) - parms["b"] * x1
        dx2 <- sin(x3) - parms["b"] * x2
        dx3 <- sin(x1) - parms["b"] * x3
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]; x3 <- X[, 3]
        cbind(
          dx1 = sin(x2) - parms["b"] * x1,
          dx2 = sin(x3) - parms["b"] * x2,
          dx3 = sin(x1) - parms["b"] * x3
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "sin(x2)"),
        c("x2", "sin(x3)"),
        c("x3", "sin(x1)")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = -0.18, "sin(x2)" = 1.0),
        c("x2" = -0.18, "sin(x3)" = 1.0),
        c("x3" = -0.18, "sin(x1)" = 1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = c("sin(x1)", "sin(x2)", "sin(x3)", "cos(x1)", "cos(x2)", "cos(x3)")
    ),

    sys11_goodwin_mm = list(
      sys_id = 11L,
      id = "sys11_goodwin_mm",
      name = "Michaelis-Menten Goodwin Oscillator",
      group = "Group_B_NonPolynomial",
      dim = 3L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 0.5, x2 = 0.5, x3 = 0.5),
      parameters = c(p = 2.0, v0 = 1.0, k1 = 0.5, k2 = 0.5, vm = 1.0, Km = 1.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 40.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x1 <- state[1]; x2 <- state[2]; x3 <- state[3]
        dx1 <- parms["v0"] / (1.0 + x3^2) - parms["k1"] * x1
        dx2 <- parms["k1"] * x1 - parms["k2"] * x2
        dx3 <- parms["k2"] * x2 - (parms["vm"] * x3) / (parms["Km"] + abs(x3))
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        x1 <- X[, 1]; x2 <- X[, 2]; x3 <- X[, 3]
        cbind(
          dx1 = parms["v0"] / (1.0 + x3^2) - parms["k1"] * x1,
          dx2 = parms["k1"] * x1 - parms["k2"] * x2,
          dx3 = parms["k2"] * x2 - (parms["vm"] * x3) / (parms["Km"] + abs(x3))
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "1/(1+x3^2)"),
        c("x1", "x2"),
        c("x2", "x3/(1+x3)")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = -0.5, "1/(1+x3^2)" = 1.0),
        c("x1" = 0.5, "x2" = -0.5),
        c("x2" = 0.5, "x3/(1+x3)" = -1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = c("1/(1+x1^2)", "1/(1+x2^2)", "1/(1+x3^2)", "x1/(1+x1)", "x2/(1+x2)", "x3/(1+x3)")
    ),

    sys12_lorenz96_5d = list(
      sys_id = 12L,
      id = "sys12_lorenz96_5d",
      name = "Lorenz-96 (5D Coupled)",
      group = "Group_C_HighDim",
      dim = 5L,
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3", "x4", "x5"),
      initial_conditions = c(x1 = 8.0, x2 = 8.0, x3 = 8.0, x4 = 8.0, x5 = 8.01),
      parameters = c(F = 8.0),
      dt = 0.0001,
      T_max = 30.0,
      rhs = function(t, state, parms) {
        x <- state
        F_val <- parms["F"]
        dx1 <- (x[2] - x[4]) * x[5] - x[1] + F_val
        dx2 <- (x[3] - x[5]) * x[1] - x[2] + F_val
        dx3 <- (x[4] - x[1]) * x[2] - x[3] + F_val
        dx4 <- (x[5] - x[2]) * x[3] - x[4] + F_val
        dx5 <- (x[1] - x[3]) * x[4] - x[5] + F_val
        list(c(dx1, dx2, dx3, dx4, dx5))
      },
      analytical_deriv = function(X, parms) {
        F_val <- parms["F"]
        cbind(
          dx1 = (X[, 2] - X[, 4]) * X[, 5] - X[, 1] + F_val,
          dx2 = (X[, 3] - X[, 5]) * X[, 1] - X[, 2] + F_val,
          dx3 = (X[, 4] - X[, 1]) * X[, 2] - X[, 3] + F_val,
          dx4 = (X[, 5] - X[, 2]) * X[, 3] - X[, 4] + F_val,
          dx5 = (X[, 1] - X[, 3]) * X[, 4] - X[, 5] + F_val
        )
      },
      ground_truth = list(
        c("x1", "x2*x5", "x4*x5"),
        c("x2", "x1*x3", "x1*x5"),
        c("x3", "x1*x2", "x2*x4"),
        c("x4", "x2*x3", "x3*x5"),
        c("x5", "x1*x4", "x3*x4")
      ),
      true_coefs = list(
        c("x1" = -1.0, "x2*x5" = 1.0, "x4*x5" = -1.0),
        c("x2" = -1.0, "x1*x3" = 1.0, "x1*x5" = -1.0),
        c("x3" = -1.0, "x1*x2" = -1.0, "x2*x4" = 1.0),
        c("x4" = -1.0, "x2*x3" = -1.0, "x3*x5" = 1.0),
        c("x5" = -1.0, "x1*x4" = 1.0, "x3*x4" = -1.0)
      ),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    )
  )
}

#' Finite difference derivative helper (compatible with original IWSM scripts)
finite_difference_matrix <- function(X, dt) {
  dX <- apply(X, 2, diff) / dt
  dX
}

#' Savitzky-Golay + Smoothing Spline numerical derivative estimator
estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline <- function(X, times, sg_p = 3, sg_n = 7) {
  dt <- median(diff(times))
  n_obs <- nrow(X)
  m <- ncol(X)
  dX <- matrix(0, nrow = n_obs, ncol = m)
  colnames(dX) <- paste0("d", colnames(X))

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    y_col <- X[, j]
    sp_fit <- tryCatch(
      stats::smooth.spline(x = times, y = y_col, spar = 0.30),
      error = function(e) NULL
    )
    if (!is.null(sp_fit)) {
      d_sp <- stats::predict(sp_fit, x = times, deriv = 1)$y
      if (n_obs >= sg_n && sg_n %% 2 == 1) {
        d_sg <- tryCatch(
          signal::sgolayfilt(y_col, p = sg_p, n = sg_n, m = 1) / dt,
          error = function(e) d_sp
        )
        dX[, j] <- 0.5 * (d_sp + d_sg)
      } else {
        dX[, j] <- d_sp
      }
    } else {
      # Central difference fallback
      d_col <- numeric(n_obs)
      d_col[1] <- (y_col[2] - y_col[1]) / dt
      d_col[n_obs] <- (y_col[n_obs] - y_col[n_obs - 1]) / dt
      if (n_obs > 2) {
        d_col[2:(n_obs - 1)] <- (y_col[3:n_obs] - y_col[1:(n_obs - 2)]) / (2 * dt)
      }
      dX[, j] <- d_col
    }
  }
  dX
}

#' Total Variation Regularized Differentiation (Chartrand 2011 TVRegDiff in R)
estimate_derivatives_tvregdiff <- function(X, times, alpha = 1e-2, n_iter = 15) {
  dt <- median(diff(times))
  n_obs <- nrow(X)
  m <- ncol(X)
  dX <- matrix(0, nrow = n_obs, ncol = m)
  colnames(dX) <- paste0("d", colnames(X))

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    u <- X[, j]
    # Initialize with central difference
    du <- numeric(n_obs)
    du[1] <- (u[2] - u[1]) / dt
    du[n_obs] <- (u[n_obs] - u[n_obs - 1]) / dt
    if (n_obs > 2) {
      du[2:(n_obs - 1)] <- (u[3:n_obs] - u[1:(n_obs - 2)]) / (2 * dt)
    }
    # Iterative TV-penalized tridiagonal smoothing of derivative
    eps_tv <- 1e-4 * max(sd(du), 1e-6)
    for (iter in seq_len(n_iter)) {
      d2 <- diff(du)
      w <- 1.0 / sqrt(d2^2 + eps_tv^2)
      # Build penalized normal equations: (I + alpha * D^T W D) du_new = du_init
      diag_main <- rep(1.0, n_obs)
      diag_main[1:(n_obs - 1)] <- diag_main[1:(n_obs - 1)] + alpha * w
      diag_main[2:n_obs] <- diag_main[2:n_obs] + alpha * w
      off_diag <- -alpha * w
      M <- diag(diag_main)
      M[cbind(1:(n_obs - 1), 2:n_obs)] <- off_diag
      M[cbind(2:n_obs, 1:(n_obs - 1))] <- off_diag
      du <- tryCatch(solve(M, du), error = function(e) du)
    }
    dX[, j] <- du
  }
  dX
}

# In-memory cache environment for base noiseless trajectories
.traj_cache <- new.env(parent = emptyenv())

#' Simulate or retrieve cached fine-grid trajectory for a synthetic ODE system
simulate_base_trajectory <- function(system_id, dt = NULL, T_max = NULL, use_cache = TRUE) {
  reg <- get_systems_registry()
  if (!system_id %in% names(reg)) {
    stop("Unknown system_id: ", system_id)
  }
  sys <- reg[[system_id]]
  dt_use <- if (is.null(dt)) sys$dt else dt
  T_use <- if (is.null(T_max)) sys$T_max else T_max
  cache_key <- paste0(system_id, "_dt", dt_use, "_T", T_use)

  if (use_cache && exists(cache_key, envir = .traj_cache)) {
    return(get(cache_key, envir = .traj_cache))
  }

  times <- seq(0, T_use, by = dt_use)
  sol <- deSolve::ode(
    y = sys$initial_conditions,
    times = times,
    func = sys$rhs,
    parms = sys$parameters,
    method = "lsoda",
    rtol = 1e-9,
    atol = 1e-9
  )
  sol_mat <- as.matrix(sol)
  X_full <- sol_mat[, -1, drop = FALSE]
  colnames(X_full) <- sys$state_names

  # Finite difference derivative (aligned with X_full[-1, ])
  dX_fd <- finite_difference_matrix(X_full, dt_use)
  # Analytical derivative at X_full[-1, ]
  X_aligned <- X_full[-1, , drop = FALSE]
  t_aligned <- sol_mat[-1, 1]
  dX_analytical <- sys$analytical_deriv(X_aligned, sys$parameters)
  colnames(dX_analytical) <- paste0("d", sys$state_names)
  colnames(dX_fd) <- paste0("d", sys$state_names)

  out <- list(
    system = sys,
    times = t_aligned,
    X = X_aligned,
    dX_analytical = dX_analytical,
    dX_fd = dX_fd,
    dt = dt_use,
    T_max = T_use
  )
  if (use_cache) {
    assign(cache_key, out, envir = .traj_cache)
  }
  out
}

#' Generate a complete synthetic benchmark task split (Train, In-Sample Test, OOD Test)
generate_synthetic_task_data <- function(
  system_id,
  N_train = 500,
  noise_eta = 0.05,
  noise_regime = c("derivative_snr", "state_noise", "iwsm_absolute"),
  seed = 10101,
  N_test_in = 1000,
  N_test_ood = 1000,
  dt = NULL,
  T_max = NULL
) {
  noise_regime <- match.arg(noise_regime)
  base <- simulate_base_trajectory(system_id, dt = dt, T_max = T_max, use_cache = TRUE)
  n_total <- nrow(base$X)
  T_total <- base$T_max
  t_vec <- base$times

  # Define interior domain (0.05 T, 0.99 T) and boundary OOD domain [0, 0.05 T] U [0.99 T, T]
  idx_interior <- which(t_vec > 0.05 * T_total & t_vec < 0.99 * T_total)
  idx_ood_pool <- which(t_vec <= 0.05 * T_total | t_vec >= 0.99 * T_total)

  set.seed(as.integer(seed))

  # Sample train and non-overlapping in-sample test indices from interior
  n_needed_int <- min(length(idx_interior), N_train + N_test_in)
  sampled_int <- sample(idx_interior, size = n_needed_int, replace = FALSE)
  idx_train <- sort(sampled_int[seq_len(min(N_train, length(sampled_int)))])
  idx_test_in <- sort(sampled_int[(length(idx_train) + 1):length(sampled_int)])

  # Sample OOD test indices from boundary pool
  idx_ood_early <- which(t_vec <= 0.05 * T_total)
  idx_ood_late <- which(t_vec >= 0.99 * T_total)
  n_early <- min(length(idx_ood_early), floor(N_test_ood / 2))
  n_late <- min(length(idx_ood_late), N_test_ood - n_early)
  idx_test_ood <- sort(c(
    sample(idx_ood_early, size = n_early, replace = FALSE),
    sample(idx_ood_late, size = n_late, replace = FALSE)
  ))

  X_clean <- base$X
  Y_clean <- base$dX_fd
  m <- ncol(X_clean)

  if (noise_regime == "derivative_snr") {
    sd_deriv <- apply(Y_clean[idx_interior, , drop = FALSE], 2, sd)
    sd_deriv[sd_deriv < 1e-8] <- 1.0
    noise_sd_vec <- noise_eta * sd_deriv
    eps_train <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(length(idx_train), 0, noise_sd_vec[j]))
    eps_in <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(length(idx_test_in), 0, noise_sd_vec[j]))
    eps_ood <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(length(idx_test_ood), 0, noise_sd_vec[j]))
    X_train <- X_clean[idx_train, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_train <- Y_clean[idx_train, , drop = FALSE] + eps_train
    X_test_in <- X_clean[idx_test_in, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_test_in_noisy <- Y_clean[idx_test_in, , drop = FALSE] + eps_in
    X_test_ood <- X_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_test_ood_noisy <- Y_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE] + eps_ood
  } else if (noise_regime == "iwsm_absolute") {
    noise_sd_vec <- rep(noise_eta, m)
    eps_train <- matrix(rnorm(length(idx_train) * m, 0, noise_eta), nrow = length(idx_train), ncol = m)
    eps_in <- matrix(rnorm(length(idx_test_in) * m, 0, noise_eta), nrow = length(idx_test_in), ncol = m)
    eps_ood <- matrix(rnorm(length(idx_test_ood) * m, 0, noise_eta), nrow = length(idx_test_ood), ncol = m)
    X_train <- X_clean[idx_train, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_train <- Y_clean[idx_train, , drop = FALSE] + eps_train
    X_test_in <- X_clean[idx_test_in, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_test_in_noisy <- Y_clean[idx_test_in, , drop = FALSE] + eps_in
    X_test_ood <- X_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_test_ood_noisy <- Y_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE] + eps_ood
  } else if (noise_regime == "state_noise") {
    # Subsample an equispaced trajectory segment of size 2500 from interior, add state noise, and differentiate
    idx_sub <- round(seq(min(idx_interior), max(idx_interior), length.out = max(N_train + N_test_in, 2000)))
    t_sub <- t_vec[idx_sub]
    X_sub_clean <- X_clean[idx_sub, , drop = FALSE]
    sd_state <- apply(X_sub_clean, 2, sd)
    sd_state[sd_state < 1e-8] <- 1.0
    noise_sd_vec <- noise_eta * sd_state
    nu_mat <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(nrow(X_sub_clean), 0, noise_sd_vec[j]))
    X_sub_noisy <- X_sub_clean + nu_mat
    Y_sub_est <- estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline(X_sub_noisy, t_sub)

    perm <- sample.int(nrow(X_sub_noisy))
    tr_loc <- sort(perm[seq_len(min(N_train, length(perm)))])
    in_loc <- sort(perm[(length(tr_loc) + 1):min(length(tr_loc) + N_test_in, length(perm))])

    X_train <- X_sub_noisy[tr_loc, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_train <- Y_sub_est[tr_loc, , drop = FALSE]
    idx_train <- idx_sub[tr_loc]

    X_test_in <- X_clean[idx_sub[in_loc], , drop = FALSE]
    Y_test_in_noisy <- Y_sub_est[in_loc, , drop = FALSE]
    idx_test_in <- idx_sub[in_loc]

    X_test_ood <- X_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE]
    Y_test_ood_noisy <- Y_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE]
  }

  # Rollout validation segment (continuous trajectory segment from test domain)
  rollout_start_idx <- min(idx_ood_late)
  rollout_indices <- seq(rollout_start_idx, min(n_total, rollout_start_idx + 5000), by = 10)

  list(
    system = base$system,
    seed = seed,
    N_train = nrow(X_train),
    noise_eta = noise_eta,
    noise_regime = noise_regime,
    noise_sd_vec = noise_sd_vec,
    X_train = X_train,
    Y_train = Y_train,
    Y_train_clean = Y_clean[idx_train, , drop = FALSE],
    X_test_in = X_test_in,
    Y_test_in_clean = Y_clean[idx_test_in, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_test_in_noisy = Y_test_in_noisy,
    X_test_ood = X_test_ood,
    Y_test_ood_clean = Y_clean[idx_test_ood, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_test_ood_noisy = Y_test_ood_noisy,
    rollout = list(
      times = t_vec[rollout_indices] - t_vec[rollout_indices[1]],
      X_true = X_clean[rollout_indices, , drop = FALSE],
      x0 = X_clean[rollout_indices[1], ]
    )
  )
}


================================================================================
FILE: R/02_basis_builders.R
================================================================================
# ==============================================================================
# R/02_basis_builders.R
# Predefined Design Matrix Builders: Poly(d=3), Oracle, and Overcomplete
# ==============================================================================

#' Generate all polynomial monomials up to degree `max_deg` for state matrix `X`
build_poly_library <- function(X, max_deg = 3L) {
  X <- as.matrix(X)
  col_names <- colnames(X)
  if (is.null(col_names)) {
    col_names <- paste0("x", seq_len(ncol(X)))
    colnames(X) <- col_names
  }
  m <- ncol(X)
  n <- nrow(X)

  feats_list <- list()
  feat_names <- character(0)

  # Degree 1
  for (i in seq_len(m)) {
    feats_list[[length(feats_list) + 1L]] <- X[, i]
    feat_names <- c(feat_names, col_names[i])
  }

  # Degree 2
  if (max_deg >= 2L) {
    for (i in seq_len(m)) {
      for (j in i:m) {
        feats_list[[length(feats_list) + 1L]] <- X[, i] * X[, j]
        if (i == j) {
          feat_names <- c(feat_names, paste0(col_names[i], "^2"))
        } else {
          feat_names <- c(feat_names, paste0(col_names[i], "*", col_names[j]))
        }
      }
    }
  }

  # Degree 3
  if (max_deg >= 3L) {
    for (i in seq_len(m)) {
      for (j in i:m) {
        for (k in j:m) {
          feats_list[[length(feats_list) + 1L]] <- X[, i] * X[, j] * X[, k]
          idx <- c(i, j, k)
          tbl <- table(idx)
          parts <- character(0)
          for (v_idx in as.integer(names(tbl))) {
            pwr <- tbl[[as.character(v_idx)]]
            if (pwr == 1L) {
              parts <- c(parts, col_names[v_idx])
            } else {
              parts <- c(parts, paste0(col_names[v_idx], "^", pwr))
            }
          }
          feat_names <- c(feat_names, paste(parts, collapse = "*"))
        }
      }
    }
  }

  Phi <- do.call(cbind, feats_list)
  colnames(Phi) <- feat_names
  Phi
}

#' Evaluate a named symbolic basis atom on state matrix `X`
evaluate_basis_atom <- function(atom_str, X) {
  df <- as.data.frame(X)
  # Safe helpers for fractional power and rationals on states
  env <- list2env(as.list(df), parent = baseenv())
  env$sqrt <- function(z) base::sqrt(abs(z))
  # Replace "1/(1+x" or "+x" denominators with safe abs() if negative states occur
  val <- tryCatch(
    eval(parse(text = atom_str), envir = env),
    error = function(e) rep(0.0, nrow(X))
  )
  val[!is.finite(val)] <- 0.0
  as.numeric(val)
}

#' Build Known Correct (Oracle) Basis: Degree-3 Polynomials + Exact Ground-Truth Nonlinear Terms
build_oracle_library <- function(X, extra_atoms = character(0), max_deg = 3L) {
  Phi_poly <- build_poly_library(X, max_deg = max_deg)
  existing_names <- colnames(Phi_poly)
  extra_atoms <- setdiff(unique(extra_atoms), existing_names)
  if (length(extra_atoms) == 0L) {
    return(Phi_poly)
  }
  extra_cols <- lapply(extra_atoms, function(a) evaluate_basis_atom(a, X))
  Phi_extra <- do.call(cbind, extra_cols)
  colnames(Phi_extra) <- extra_atoms
  cbind(Phi_poly, Phi_extra)
}

#' Build Brute-Force Overcomplete Library (q > 60 columns: Poly(3) + Trig + Exp + Rational + Fractional + Cross-Terms)
build_overcomplete_library <- function(X, extra_atoms = character(0)) {
  X <- as.matrix(X)
  col_names <- colnames(X)
  if (is.null(col_names)) {
    col_names <- paste0("x", seq_len(ncol(X)))
    colnames(X) <- col_names
  }
  m <- ncol(X)
  Phi_poly <- build_poly_library(X, max_deg = 3L)

  extra_list <- list()
  extra_names <- character(0)
  add_col <- function(vec, nm) {
    if (!nm %in% c(colnames(Phi_poly), extra_names)) {
      vec[!is.finite(vec)] <- 0.0
      extra_list[[length(extra_list) + 1L]] <<- as.numeric(vec)
      extra_names <<- c(extra_names, nm)
    }
  }

  # Unary non-polynomials for every state variable
  for (i in seq_len(m)) {
    xi <- X[, i]
    nm <- col_names[i]
    add_col(sin(xi), paste0("sin(", nm, ")"))
    add_col(cos(xi), paste0("cos(", nm, ")"))
    add_col(sin(2 * xi), paste0("sin(2*", nm, ")"))
    add_col(cos(2 * xi), paste0("cos(2*", nm, ")"))
    add_col(sin(3 * xi), paste0("sin(3*", nm, ")"))
    add_col(cos(3 * xi), paste0("cos(3*", nm, ")"))
    add_col(exp(-abs(xi)), paste0("exp(-abs(", nm, "))"))
    add_col(1.0 / (1.0 + abs(xi)), paste0("1/(1+", nm, ")"))
    add_col(1.0 / (1.0 + xi^2), paste0("1/(1+", nm, "^2)"))
    add_col(xi / (1.0 + abs(xi)), paste0(nm, "/(1+", nm, ")"))
    add_col((xi^2) / (1.0 + xi^2), paste0(nm, "^2/(1+", nm, "^2)"))
    add_col(sqrt(abs(xi)), paste0("sqrt(", nm, ")"))
    add_col(1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-pmin(pmax(xi, -20), 20))), paste0("sigmoid(", nm, ")"))
    add_col(xi^4, paste0(nm, "^4"))
  }

  # Cross-interactions between states and non-polynomials to ensure > 60 collinear features
  for (i in seq_len(m)) {
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      xi <- X[, i]; xj <- X[, j]
      ni <- col_names[i]; nj <- col_names[j]
      add_col(xi * sin(xj), paste0(ni, "*sin(", nj, ")"))
      add_col(xi * cos(xj), paste0(ni, "*cos(", nj, ")"))
      add_col((xi * xj) / (1.0 + abs(xi)), paste0(ni, "*", nj, "/(1+", ni, ")"))
      add_col((xi * xj^2) / (1.0 + xj^2), paste0(ni, "*", nj, "^2/(1+", nj, "^2)"))
      add_col(xi * exp(-abs(xj)), paste0(ni, "*exp(-abs(", nj, "))"))
      add_col(sin(xi) * cos(xj), paste0("sin(", ni, ")*cos(", nj, ")"))
    }
  }

  for (a in extra_atoms) {
    add_col(evaluate_basis_atom(a, X), a)
  }

  Phi_extra <- do.call(cbind, extra_list)
  colnames(Phi_extra) <- extra_names
  cbind(Phi_poly, Phi_extra)
}

# ==============================================================================
# R/03_fbms_wrappers.R
# Tuned BGNLM (gmjmcmc.parallel) & Predefined-Basis BGLM (mjmcmc.parallel) Wrappers
# ==============================================================================

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(FBMS)
  library(yaml)
})

if (!exists("build_poly_library", mode = "function")) {
  if (file.exists("R/02_basis_builders.R")) source("R/02_basis_builders.R")
  else if (file.exists("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")) source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
}
if (!exists("extract_canonical_atoms", mode = "function")) {
  if (file.exists("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
  else if (file.exists("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
}

#' Register radian-accurate physical operator transformations in .GlobalEnv for FBMS
#' and patch FBMS:::check.collinearity for low-dimensional ODE state spaces (m = 2, 3, 5)
register_fbms_transforms <- function() {
  assign("sin_rad", function(x) sin(x), envir = .GlobalEnv)
  assign("cos_rad", function(x) cos(x), envir = .GlobalEnv)
  assign("exp_neg", function(x) exp(-abs(x)), envir = .GlobalEnv)
  assign("inv_pos", function(x) 1.0 / (1.0 + abs(x)), envir = .GlobalEnv)
  assign("inv_sq",  function(x) 1.0 / (1.0 + x^2), envir = .GlobalEnv)
  assign("hill_2",  function(x) (x^2) / (1.0 + x^2), envir = .GlobalEnv)

  # Patch FBMS:::check.collinearity so n >= 3 * length(features) + 10 for low-D dynamical systems
  fix_col <- function(proposal, features, F.0.size, data, mock) {
    features[[length(features) + 1L]] <- proposal
    n <- min(nrow(data$x), max(F.0.size * 2L, length(features) * 3L + 15L))
    if (mock) {
      mock.data <- list(
        x = matrix(runif(n * (F.0.size + data$fixed), -100, 100), n, F.0.size + data$fixed),
        y = matrix(runif(n * ncol(as.matrix(data$y)), -100, 100), n, ncol(as.matrix(data$y))),
        fixed = data$fixed
      )
    } else {
      obs_idx <- seq_len(n)
      mock.data <- list(
        x = data$x[obs_idx, , drop = FALSE],
        y = as.matrix(data$y)[obs_idx, , drop = FALSE],
        fixed = data$fixed
      )
    }
    mock.data.precalc <- FBMS:::precalc.features(mock.data, features)
    linearmod <- lm.fit(mock.data.precalc$x, mock.data.precalc$y)
    if (sum(is.na(linearmod$coefficients)) == 0L) return(FALSE) else return(TRUE)
  }
  environment(fix_col) <- asNamespace("FBMS")
  tryCatch(assignInNamespace("check.collinearity", fix_col, ns = "FBMS"), error = function(e) NULL)

  invisible(list(
    transforms_core = c("sin_rad", "cos_rad", "p2", "p3", "inv_pos", "inv_sq"),
    transforms_extended = c("sin_rad", "cos_rad", "p2", "p3", "p05", "pm1", "exp_neg", "inv_pos", "inv_sq", "hill_2", "sigmoid"),
    transforms_iwsm = c("sin_deg", "cos_deg", "p0", "p2", "p3", "p05", "pm1", "pm2", "pm05")
  ))
}

#' Load Tuned BGNLM Hyperparameters from config/best_bgnlm_params.yaml (or return defaults)
get_tuned_bgnlm_config <- function(N_obs = 500L, p_cov = 3L) {
  cfg_paths <- c("config/best_bgnlm_params.yaml", "../../config/best_bgnlm_params.yaml")
  cfg_file <- cfg_paths[file.exists(cfg_paths)][1]

  if (!is.na(cfg_file) && file.exists(cfg_file)) {
    cfg <- yaml::read_yaml(cfg_file)
  } else {
    cfg <- list(
      beta_prior_type = "g-prior",
      g_rule = "max_n_p2",
      var = "unknown",
      r_rule = "N^(-1.0)",
      p_inter = 0.55,
      p_mod = 0.35,
      p_proj = 0.00,
      p_new = 0.10,
      pop_max = 20L,
      max_depth_D = 2L,
      P_generations = 15L,
      N_mcmc = 500L,
      runs = 6L,
      transforms_tier = "transforms_extended",
      energy_prune_tol = 0.025
    )
  }

  # Evaluate beta_prior
  if (cfg$beta_prior_type == "g-prior") {
    g_val <- max(as.numeric(N_obs), as.numeric(p_cov)^2)
    beta_prior <- list(type = "g-prior", g = g_val)
  } else if (cfg$beta_prior_type == "Jeffreys-BIC") {
    beta_prior <- list(type = "Jeffreys-BIC", var = cfg$var)
  } else {
    beta_prior <- list(type = cfg$beta_prior_type)
  }

  # Evaluate complexity penalty r
  N <- as.numeric(N_obs)
  r_val <- switch(
    as.character(cfg$r_rule),
    "N^(-0.5)" = N^(-0.5),
    "N^(-1.0)" = N^(-1.0),
    "N^(-1.5)" = N^(-1.5),
    "exp(-2)"  = exp(-2.0),
    "exp(-4)"  = exp(-4.0),
    1.0 / N
  )

  list(
    beta_prior = beta_prior,
    model_prior = list(r = r_val),
    p_inter = as.numeric(cfg$p_inter),
    p_mod = as.numeric(cfg$p_mod),
    p_proj = as.numeric(cfg$p_proj),
    p_new = as.numeric(cfg$p_new),
    pop_max = as.integer(cfg$pop_max),
    max_depth_D = as.integer(cfg$max_depth_D),
    P_generations = as.integer(cfg$P_generations),
    N_mcmc = as.integer(cfg$N_mcmc),
    runs = as.integer(cfg$runs),
    transforms_tier = as.character(cfg$transforms_tier),
    energy_prune_tol = if (!is.null(cfg$energy_prune_tol)) as.numeric(cfg$energy_prune_tol) else 0.025
  )
}

#' Evaluate an FBMS feature string safely on a state/design matrix
eval_fbms_feature_string <- function(feat_str, X_df) {
  register_fbms_transforms()
  s <- trimws(as.character(feat_str))
  # Normalize x.1 -> x1 if needed
  for (k in seq_len(ncol(X_df))) {
    s <- gsub(paste0("\\bx\\.", k, "\\b"), colnames(X_df)[k], s)
  }
  env <- list2env(as.list(X_df), parent = .GlobalEnv)
  val <- tryCatch(
    eval(parse(text = s), envir = env),
    error = function(e) {
      if (s %in% colnames(X_df)) return(X_df[[s]])
      evaluate_basis_atom(s, X_df)
    }
  )
  val <- as.numeric(val)
  val[!is.finite(val)] <- 0.0
  val
}

#' Fit a single FBMS method across all m equations of a dynamical system
run_fbms_method <- function(
  method_id = c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned",
    "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned",
    "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
    "BGLM_Oracle_Basis",
    "BGLM_Poly_Basis",
    "BGLM_Overcomplete"
  ),
  task_data,
  cores = 2L,
  tuning_override = NULL,
  fast_mode = FALSE
) {
  method_id <- match.arg(method_id)
  t_dicts <- register_fbms_transforms()

  X_tr_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_train)
  Y_tr <- as.matrix(task_data$Y_train)
  X_in_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_in)
  X_ood_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_ood)
  m <- ncol(Y_tr)
  N_obs <- nrow(X_tr_raw)
  extra_oracle <- if (!is.null(task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis)) task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis else character(0)

  # Build design matrix X depending on method_id
  build_X_fn <- function(X_mat) {
    if (method_id %in% c("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned")) {
      return(X_mat)
    } else if (method_id == "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly") {
      return(build_poly_library(X_mat, max_deg = 2L))
    } else if (method_id == "BGLM_Oracle_Basis") {
      return(build_oracle_library(X_mat, extra_atoms = extra_oracle, max_deg = 3L))
    } else if (method_id == "BGLM_Poly_Basis") {
      return(build_poly_library(X_mat, max_deg = 3L))
    } else if (method_id == "BGLM_Overcomplete") {
      return(build_overcomplete_library(X_mat, extra_atoms = extra_oracle))
    }
  }

  X_tr_full <- build_X_fn(X_tr_raw)
  X_in_full <- build_X_fn(X_in_raw)
  X_ood_full <- build_X_fn(X_ood_raw)

  # Drop constant / zero-variance columns so FBMS:::check.data() never fails
  active_cols <- which(apply(X_tr_full, 2, function(z) {
    s <- suppressWarnings(sd(z))
    is.finite(s) && s > 1e-12 && any(z != z[1])
  }))
  if (length(active_cols) == 0L) {
    active_cols <- seq_len(min(1L, ncol(X_tr_full)))
  }
  X_tr <- X_tr_full[, active_cols, drop = FALSE]
  X_in <- X_in_full[, active_cols, drop = FALSE]
  X_ood <- X_ood_full[, active_cols, drop = FALSE]
  orig_labels <- colnames(X_tr)

  # Safe R syntactic column names for FBMS formula interface, mapped back via orig_labels
  safe_cols <- paste0("v", seq_len(ncol(X_tr)))
  X_tr_df <- as.data.frame(X_tr); colnames(X_tr_df) <- safe_cols
  X_in_df <- as.data.frame(X_in); colnames(X_in_df) <- safe_cols
  X_ood_df <- as.data.frame(X_ood); colnames(X_ood_df) <- safe_cols

  cfg <- get_tuned_bgnlm_config(N_obs = N_obs, p_cov = ncol(X_tr))
  if (!is.null(tuning_override)) {
    for (nm in names(tuning_override)) cfg[[nm]] <- tuning_override[[nm]]
  }
  if (fast_mode) {
    cfg$P_generations <- min(cfg$P_generations, 6L)
    cfg$N_mcmc <- min(cfg$N_mcmc, 250L)
    cfg$runs <- min(cfg$runs, 2L)
  }

  discovered_mpm <- vector("list", m)
  discovered_map <- vector("list", m)
  pip_list <- vector("list", m)
  eq_models <- vector("list", m)

  pred_train <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_tr), ncol = m)
  pred_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_in), ncol = m)
  pred_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_ood), ncol = m)
  lower_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_in), ncol = m)
  upper_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_in), ncol = m)
  lower_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_ood), ncol = m)
  upper_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_ood), ncol = m)

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    df_j <- cbind(yc = as.numeric(Y_tr[, j]), X_tr_df)
    form_j <- as.formula(paste("yc ~", paste(safe_cols, collapse = " + ")))

    if (method_id == "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned") {
      # Exact IWSM baseline settings (untuned)
      params <- FBMS::gen.params.gmjmcmc(ncol(X_tr_df))
      params$feat$pop.max <- 15L
      params$feat$keep.org <- TRUE
      params$feat$check.col <- TRUE
      P_use <- if (fast_mode) 5L else 12L
      N_use <- if (fast_mode) 200L else 400L
      runs_use <- if (fast_mode) 2L else 4L
      fbms_fit <- suppressWarnings(FBMS::fbms(
        formula = form_j,
        family = "gaussian",
        beta_prior = list(type = "Jeffreys-BIC", var = 1.0),
        model_prior = list(r = 1.0 / nrow(df_j)),
        data = df_j,
        method = "gmjmcmc.parallel",
        params = params,
        runs = runs_use,
        cores = max(1L, as.integer(cores)),
        N = N_use,
        P = P_use,
        transforms = t_dicts$transforms_iwsm,
        verbose = FALSE
      ))
      prune_tol <- 1e-4 # Untuned IWSM did not prune weak features
    } else if (method_id %in% c("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly")) {
      transforms_vec <- if (cfg$transforms_tier == "transforms_core") t_dicts$transforms_core else t_dicts$transforms_extended
      params <- FBMS::gen.params.gmjmcmc(ncol(X_tr_df))
      params$feat$pop.max <- cfg$pop_max
      params$feat$D <- cfg$max_depth_D
      params$feat$keep.org <- TRUE
      params$feat$check.col <- TRUE
      params$feat$eps <- 0.20
      probs <- FBMS::gen.probs.gmjmcmc(transforms_vec)
      probs$gen <- c(cfg$p_inter, cfg$p_mod, cfg$p_proj, cfg$p_new)

      use_custom_g <- identical(cfg$beta_prior$type, "g-prior")
      fbms_fit <- suppressWarnings(FBMS::fbms(
        formula = form_j,
        family = if (use_custom_g) "custom" else "gaussian",
        loglik.pi = if (use_custom_g) FBMS:::gaussian.loglik.g else NULL,
        beta_prior = if (use_custom_g) list(g = cfg$beta_prior$g) else cfg$beta_prior,
        model_prior = cfg$model_prior,
        data = df_j,
        method = "gmjmcmc.parallel",
        params = params,
        probs = probs,
        runs = cfg$runs,
        cores = max(1L, as.integer(cores)),
        N = cfg$N_mcmc,
        P = cfg$P_generations,
        transforms = transforms_vec,
        verbose = FALSE
      ))
      prune_tol <- cfg$energy_prune_tol
    } else {
      # Predefined-Basis BGLM via mjmcmc.parallel
      N_mcmc_bglm <- if (fast_mode) 400L else max(800L, cfg$N_mcmc)
      runs_bglm <- if (fast_mode) 2L else max(4L, cfg$runs)
      use_custom_g <- identical(cfg$beta_prior$type, "g-prior")
      fbms_fit <- suppressWarnings(FBMS::fbms(
        formula = form_j,
        family = if (use_custom_g) "custom" else "gaussian",
        loglik.pi = if (use_custom_g) FBMS:::gaussian.loglik.g else NULL,
        beta_prior = if (use_custom_g) list(g = cfg$beta_prior$g) else cfg$beta_prior,
        model_prior = cfg$model_prior,
        data = df_j,
        method = "mjmcmc.parallel",
        runs = runs_bglm,
        cores = max(1L, as.integer(cores)),
        N = N_mcmc_bglm,
        verbose = FALSE
      ))
      prune_tol <- cfg$energy_prune_tol
    }

    # Extract full PIP table with original column labels
    sum_all <- suppressWarnings(summary(fbms_fit, tol = 0.0, labels = orig_labels, verbose = FALSE))
    feat_strs_all <- as.character(sum_all$feats.strings)
    pips_all <- as.numeric(sum_all$marg.probs)
    names(pips_all) <- feat_strs_all
    pip_list[[j]] <- pips_all

    # Extract MAP model via get.best.model
    best_mod <- tryCatch(
      suppressWarnings(FBMS::get.best.model(fbms_fit, labels = orig_labels)),
      error = function(e) NULL
    )
    if (!is.null(best_mod) && length(best_mod$coefs) > 1L) {
      active_map <- names(best_mod$coefs)[-1][best_mod$coefs[-1] != 0]
      discovered_map[[j]] <- extract_canonical_atoms(active_map)
    } else {
      discovered_map[[j]] <- character(0)
    }

    # Extract MPM features (PIP > 0.5)
    mpm_idx <- which(pips_all > 0.5)
    mpm_raw_feats <- feat_strs_all[mpm_idx]

    # Evaluate selected MPM features on train/in/ood and refit posterior mode / OLS with energy pruning
    if (length(mpm_raw_feats) > 0L) {
      X_tr_named <- as.data.frame(X_tr); colnames(X_tr_named) <- orig_labels
      Phi_mpm_tr <- sapply(mpm_raw_feats, function(fs) eval_fbms_feature_string(fs, X_tr_named))
      if (!is.matrix(Phi_mpm_tr)) Phi_mpm_tr <- matrix(Phi_mpm_tr, nrow = nrow(X_tr_named))
      colnames(Phi_mpm_tr) <- mpm_raw_feats

      # Energy pruning of negligible MPM terms
      fit_init <- lm.fit(x = cbind(1.0, Phi_mpm_tr), y = Y_tr[, j])
      coefs_init <- fit_init$coefficients[-1]
      coefs_init[is.na(coefs_init)] <- 0.0
      y_norm <- max(sqrt(sum(Y_tr[, j]^2)), 1e-12)
      rel_energy <- sapply(seq_along(mpm_raw_feats), function(k) {
        abs(coefs_init[k]) * sqrt(sum(Phi_mpm_tr[, k]^2)) / y_norm
      })
      keep_k <- which(rel_energy >= prune_tol)
      mpm_kept_feats <- mpm_raw_feats[keep_k]
    } else {
      mpm_kept_feats <- character(0)
    }

    discovered_mpm[[j]] <- extract_canonical_atoms(mpm_kept_feats)

    # Fit final linear predictor on kept MPM features + compute BMA predictive intervals
    X_tr_named <- as.data.frame(X_tr); colnames(X_tr_named) <- orig_labels
    X_in_named <- as.data.frame(X_in); colnames(X_in_named) <- orig_labels
    X_ood_named <- as.data.frame(X_ood); colnames(X_ood_named) <- orig_labels

    if (length(mpm_kept_feats) > 0L) {
      Phi_tr <- as.matrix(sapply(mpm_kept_feats, function(fs) eval_fbms_feature_string(fs, X_tr_named)))
      Phi_in <- as.matrix(sapply(mpm_kept_feats, function(fs) eval_fbms_feature_string(fs, X_in_named)))
      Phi_ood <- as.matrix(sapply(mpm_kept_feats, function(fs) eval_fbms_feature_string(fs, X_ood_named)))
      if (nrow(X_tr_named) == 1L) Phi_tr <- matrix(Phi_tr, nrow = 1L)
      if (nrow(X_in_named) == 1L) Phi_in <- matrix(Phi_in, nrow = 1L)
      if (nrow(X_ood_named) == 1L) Phi_ood <- matrix(Phi_ood, nrow = 1L)

      X_des_tr <- cbind(1.0, Phi_tr)
      X_des_in <- cbind(1.0, Phi_in)
      X_des_ood <- cbind(1.0, Phi_ood)
      fit_final <- lm.fit(x = X_des_tr, y = Y_tr[, j])
      beta_hat <- fit_final$coefficients
      beta_hat[is.na(beta_hat)] <- 0.0
      df_res <- max(1L, nrow(X_des_tr) - qr(X_des_tr)$rank)
      sigma_hat <- sqrt(sum(fit_final$residuals^2) / df_res)

      mu_tr <- as.numeric(X_des_tr %*% beta_hat)
      mu_in <- as.numeric(X_des_in %*% beta_hat)
      mu_ood <- as.numeric(X_des_ood %*% beta_hat)

      # Epistemic + Aleatoric variance for 95% credible predictive intervals
      XtX_inv <- tryCatch(chol2inv(chol(crossprod(X_des_tr) + diag(1e-6, ncol(X_des_tr)))),
                          error = function(e) diag(1.0 / nrow(X_des_tr), ncol(X_des_tr)))
      se_in <- sigma_hat * sqrt(1.0 + pmax(0.0, rowSums((X_des_in %*% XtX_inv) * X_des_in)))
      se_ood <- sigma_hat * sqrt(1.0 + pmax(0.0, rowSums((X_des_ood %*% XtX_inv) * X_des_ood)))
    } else {
      beta_hat <- mean(Y_tr[, j])
      sigma_hat <- sd(Y_tr[, j])
      mu_tr <- rep(beta_hat, nrow(X_tr))
      mu_in <- rep(beta_hat, nrow(X_in))
      mu_ood <- rep(beta_hat, nrow(X_ood))
      se_in <- rep(sigma_hat, nrow(X_in))
      se_ood <- rep(sigma_hat, nrow(X_ood))
    }

    # Combine with FBMS BMA quantiles if available
    bma_in <- tryCatch(suppressWarnings(predict(fbms_fit, x = X_in_df)), error = function(e) NULL)
    bma_ood <- tryCatch(suppressWarnings(predict(fbms_fit, x = X_ood_df)), error = function(e) NULL)

    if (!is.null(bma_in) && !is.null(bma_in$aggr$mean) && all(is.finite(bma_in$aggr$mean))) {
      # Blend BMA mean and MPM mean, and widen BMA quantiles by residual dispersion sigma_hat
      q_low_bma <- bma_in$aggr$quantiles[1, ]
      q_high_bma <- bma_in$aggr$quantiles[3, ]
      bma_halfwidth <- 0.5 * (q_high_bma - q_low_bma)
      tot_halfwidth_in <- sqrt(bma_halfwidth^2 + (1.96 * se_in)^2)
      lower_in[, j] <- mu_in - tot_halfwidth_in
      upper_in[, j] <- mu_in + tot_halfwidth_in
    } else {
      lower_in[, j] <- mu_in - 1.96 * se_in
      upper_in[, j] <- mu_in + 1.96 * se_in
    }

    if (!is.null(bma_ood) && !is.null(bma_ood$aggr$mean) && all(is.finite(bma_ood$aggr$mean))) {
      q_low_bma <- bma_ood$aggr$quantiles[1, ]
      q_high_bma <- bma_ood$aggr$quantiles[3, ]
      bma_halfwidth <- 0.5 * (q_high_bma - q_low_bma)
      tot_halfwidth_ood <- sqrt(bma_halfwidth^2 + (1.96 * se_ood)^2)
      lower_ood[, j] <- mu_ood - tot_halfwidth_ood
      upper_ood[, j] <- mu_ood + tot_halfwidth_ood
    } else {
      lower_ood[, j] <- mu_ood - 1.96 * se_ood
      upper_ood[, j] <- mu_ood + 1.96 * se_ood
    }

    pred_train[, j] <- mu_tr
    pred_in[, j] <- mu_in
    pred_ood[, j] <- mu_ood

    eq_models[[j]] <- list(
      feats = mpm_kept_feats,
      beta = beta_hat,
      sigma = sigma_hat
    )
  }

  # Construct fast RHS predictor for ODE trajectory rollout
  predict_rhs_fn <- function(X_state_mat) {
    X_full <- build_X_fn(X_state_mat)[, active_cols, drop = FALSE]
    X_df_new <- as.data.frame(X_full)
    colnames(X_df_new) <- orig_labels
    out_d <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_df_new), ncol = m)
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      mod_j <- eq_models[[j]]
      if (length(mod_j$feats) == 0L) {
        out_d[, j] <- mod_j$beta[1]
      } else {
        Phi_new <- sapply(mod_j$feats, function(fs) eval_fbms_feature_string(fs, X_df_new))
        if (nrow(X_df_new) == 1L) Phi_new <- matrix(Phi_new, nrow = 1L)
        out_d[, j] <- as.numeric(cbind(1.0, Phi_new) %*% mod_j$beta)
      }
    }
    out_d
  }

  list(
    method_id = method_id,
    discovered = discovered_mpm,
    discovered_map = discovered_map,
    pip_list = pip_list,
    pred_train = pred_train,
    pred_in = pred_in,
    pred_ood = pred_ood,
    lower_in = lower_in,
    upper_in = upper_in,
    lower_ood = lower_ood,
    upper_ood = upper_ood,
    predict_rhs_fn = predict_rhs_fn
  )
}
