#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_mixed_models.R
# Runs LMM/GLMM Statistical Evaluation & Exports LaTeX Tables and Forest Plots
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
})

source("R/08_mixed_effects_eval.R")
source("R/09_plotting_tables.R")

dir.create("results/tables", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
dir.create("results/figures", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

synth_file <- "results/aggregated/synth_results.rds"
real_file <- "results/aggregated/real_results.rds"

icc_synth <- data.table()
icc_real <- data.table()

if (file.exists(synth_file)) {
  df_synth <- readRDS(synth_file)
  message("[MIXED MODELS] Analyzing synthetic benchmark results (N = ", nrow(df_synth), " rows)...")
  res_synth <- analyze_synthetic_mixed_effects(df_synth)
  icc_synth <- res_synth$icc_table

  fwrite(res_synth$fixed_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_fixed.csv")
  if (nrow(res_synth$dunnett_contrasts) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$dunnett_contrasts, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_dunnett.csv")
  }
  if (nrow(res_synth$regime_effects) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$regime_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_regime.csv")
  }
  if (!is.null(res_synth$glmm_effects) && nrow(res_synth$glmm_effects) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$glmm_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_glmm.csv")
    export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
      res_synth$glmm_effects,
      out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_glmm.tex",
      caption_str = "Binomial GLMM Log-Odds Contrasts for Exact System Recovery $I_{\\text{exact}} \\in \\{0,1\\}$ (Reference: \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}).",
      label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_glmm"
    )
  }

  # Held-out 9-system validation LMM (excluding tuning systems sys02, sys04, sys08)
  df_held9 <- df_synth[!System %in% c("sys02_lorenz63", "sys04_lotka_volterra", "sys08_rossler_lorenz_hybrid")]
  if (nrow(df_held9) > 0L) {
    res_held9 <- analyze_synthetic_mixed_effects(df_held9)
    fwrite(res_held9$fixed_effects[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")], "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_heldout9.csv")
  }

  export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
    res_synth$fixed_effects[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")],
    out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_synth.tex",
    caption_str = "Linear Mixed-Effects Model (LMM) Contrasts on Synthetic Benchmark Suite with \\texttt{(1|System:Method)} Random Interaction (Reference: \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}).",
    label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_synth"
  )

  if (nrow(res_synth$regime_effects) > 0L) {
    export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
      res_synth$regime_effects,
      out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_regime.tex",
      caption_str = "Regime Moderation Interaction LMM (\\texttt{Method * SystemGroup}) Testing Polynomial vs.\\ Non-Polynomial vs.\\ 5D Dynamics (\\texttt{Group\\_C} comprises 5D Lorenz-96).",
      label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_regime"
    )
  }

  plot_mixed_effects_forest(
    res_synth$fixed_effects[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")],
    out_pdf = "results/figures/fig_mixed_effects_forest_synth.pdf",
    title_str = "Experiment 1 (Synthetic ODEs): Mixed-Effects Contrasts vs. BGNLM_Full_Tuned"
  )
  plot_benchmark_summary(df_synth, out_pdf = "results/figures/fig_synth_benchmark_overview.pdf")
  message("[OK] Exported synthetic mixed-effects tables and forest plots.")
}

if (file.exists(real_file)) {
  df_real <- readRDS(real_file)
  message("[MIXED MODELS] Analyzing quasi-empirical ODE dataset results (N = ", nrow(df_real), " rows)...")
  res_real <- analyze_real_mixed_effects(df_real)
  icc_real <- res_real$icc_table

  fwrite(res_real$fixed_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_real_fixed.csv")
  if (nrow(res_real$dunnett_contrasts) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_real$dunnett_contrasts, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_real_dunnett.csv")
  }

  export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
    res_real$fixed_effects,
    out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_real.tex",
    caption_str = "Linear Mixed-Effects Model (LMM) Contrasts on Quasi-Empirical Literature ODE Benchmarks with \\texttt{(1|Dataset:Method)} Random Interaction (Reference: \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}).",
    label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_real"
  )

  plot_mixed_effects_forest(
    res_real$fixed_effects,
    out_pdf = "results/figures/fig_mixed_effects_forest_real.pdf",
    title_str = "Experiment 2 (Quasi-Empirical ODEs): Mixed-Effects Contrasts vs. BGNLM_Full_Tuned"
  )
  message("[OK] Exported quasi-empirical mixed-effects tables and forest plots.")
}

if (nrow(icc_synth) > 0L || nrow(icc_real) > 0L) {
  export_icc_latex_table(icc_synth, icc_real, "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_icc.tex")
}


================================================================================
FILE: scripts/run_remote_server.sh
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Usage: ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh [SUITE: synth|real|both] [NUM_WORKERS] [THREADS_PER_WORKER] [START_TASK] [END_TASK]
# Recommended on nam-shub-01 (56 cores) via nohup:
#   nohup ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh both 40 1 > run_benchmarks.log 2>&1 &

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

SUITE=${1:-both}
NUM_WORKERS=${2:-36}
THREADS_PER_WORKER=${3:-1}
START_TASK_USER=${4:-}
END_TASK_USER=${5:-}

# Load R module ONLY if available (do NOT load Python module as it can conflict with .venv)
if command -v module >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
elif [ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if ! command -v Rscript >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERROR] Rscript not found! Run ./scripts/setup_server.sh or load R module first."
  exit 1
fi

# Enforce project-local R library (.r_lib) if present
if [ -d "$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib" ]; then
  export R_LIBS_USER="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
  export R_LIBS="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
fi

# Activate project-local Python .venv if present
if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate" ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate"
  export VIRTUAL_ENV="$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
  export PATH="$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin:$PATH"
fi

export OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
export OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1
export MKL_NUM_THREADS=1
export VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS=1
export JULIA_NUM_THREADS=1

mkdir -p results/checkpoints_synth results/checkpoints_real logs

echo "[INFO] Running pre-flight environment verification..."
Rscript scripts/verify_env.R

echo "[INFO] Ensuring real-world datasets are present..."
Rscript scripts/download_real_data.R

if [[ ! -f "config/manifest_synth.csv" || ! -f "config/manifest_real.csv" ]]; then
  echo "[INFO] Generating default manifests..."
  Rscript scripts/generate_manifest.R
fi

# Resolve OS timeout command if available (GNU coreutils on Linux)
TASK_TIMEOUT=${TASK_TIMEOUT:-600}
OS_TIMEOUT=$((TASK_TIMEOUT + 60))
TIMEOUT_CMD=""
if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  TIMEOUT_CMD="timeout -k 15 ${OS_TIMEOUT}"
fi
export TASK_TIMEOUT
export TIMEOUT_CMD

run_suite() {
  local s=$1
  local manifest="config/manifest_${s}.csv"
  local ckpt_dir="results/checkpoints_${s}"
  local total_tasks
  total_tasks=$(tail -n +2 "$manifest" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')

  local start_task=${START_TASK_USER:-1}
  local end_task=${END_TASK_USER:-$total_tasks}
  if (( end_task > total_tasks )); then
    end_task=$total_tasks
  fi

  # Emit ONLY missing task_ids in a single fast pass (< 0.5s) to avoid spawning R for completed tasks
  local pending_file="logs/.pending_${s}_tasks.txt"
  Rscript -e "
    dir_p <- '$ckpt_dir'
    existing <- list.files(dir_p, pattern = '^task_[0-9]+[.]rds$')
    done_ids <- if (length(existing) > 0L) as.integer(gsub('[^0-9]', '', existing)) else integer(0)
    all_ids <- seq.int(${start_task}L, ${end_task}L)
    todo <- setdiff(all_ids, done_ids)
    writeLines(as.character(todo), '$pending_file')
    cat(sprintf('[INFO] Suite %s: %d total in range [%d..%d], %d already done, %d pending.',
                '$s', length(all_ids), ${start_task}L, ${end_task}L, length(all_ids) - length(todo), length(todo)), '\n')
  "

  local n_pending
  n_pending=$(wc -l < "$pending_file" | tr -d ' ')
  if (( n_pending == 0 )); then
    echo "[INFO] Suite '$s' is already 100% complete!"
    return 0
  fi

  local fail_log="logs/${s}_failures.log"
  : > "$fail_log"

  echo "[INFO] Launching $n_pending pending tasks for suite '$s' across $NUM_WORKERS workers ($THREADS_PER_WORKER thread(s) each)..."
  export SUITE_CUR="$s"
  export THREADS_PER_WORKER
  export FAIL_LOG="$fail_log"

  run_one_task() {
    local tid="$1"
    local tlog="logs/${SUITE_CUR}_task_${tid}.log"
    if ${TIMEOUT_CMD} Rscript scripts/run_single_task.R --suite "${SUITE_CUR}" --task_id "${tid}" --cores "${THREADS_PER_WORKER}" --timeout "${TASK_TIMEOUT}" > "${tlog}" 2>&1; then
      rm -f "${tlog}"
    else
      local rc=$?
      echo "[FAIL] suite=${SUITE_CUR} task_id=${tid} exit=${rc} (see ${tlog})" >> "${FAIL_LOG}"
    fi
  }
  export -f run_one_task

  xargs -n 1 -P "$NUM_WORKERS" bash -c 'run_one_task "$1"' _ < "$pending_file"

  rm -f "$pending_file"
}

if [[ "$SUITE" == "synth" || "$SUITE" == "both" ]]; then
  run_suite "synth"
fi

if [[ "$SUITE" == "real" || "$SUITE" == "both" ]]; then
  run_suite "real"
fi

echo "[INFO] Aggregating checkpoints and running mixed-effects evaluation..."
Rscript scripts/aggregate_results.R
Rscript scripts/run_mixed_models.R
echo "[INFO] Pipeline complete."

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/run_single_task.R
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_single_task.R
# Atomic CLI Worker for Synthetic & Real-World Benchmark Tasks with Checkpointing
# Usage: Rscript scripts/run_single_task.R --suite <synth|real> --task_id <int> [--cores <int>]
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
get_arg <- function(flag, default = NULL) {
  idx <- which(args == flag)
  if (length(idx) > 0L && length(args) > idx[1]) return(args[idx[1] + 1L])
  default
}

suite <- get_arg("--suite", "synth")
task_id <- as.integer(get_arg("--task_id", "1"))
cores <- as.integer(get_arg("--cores", "1"))
timeout_sec <- as.numeric(get_arg("--timeout", "300"))

ckpt_dir <- paste0("results/checkpoints_", suite)
dir.create(ckpt_dir, recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
ckpt_file <- file.path(ckpt_dir, sprintf("task_%06d.rds", task_id))

# Seamless resumption BEFORE loading heavy packages or manifest
if (file.exists(ckpt_file)) {
  message("[SKIP] Checkpoint already exists: ", ckpt_file)
  quit(save = "no", status = 0L)
}

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
  library(R.utils)
})

source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
source("R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
source("R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

manifest_path <- paste0("config/manifest_", suite, ".csv")
if (!file.exists(manifest_path)) {
  stop("Manifest file does not exist: ", manifest_path)
}

manifest <- fread(manifest_path)
if (task_id < 1L || task_id > nrow(manifest)) {
  stop("Invalid task_id ", task_id, " (manifest has ", nrow(manifest), " rows).")
}

row <- manifest[task_id]
method_id <- as.character(row$method_id)
fast_mode <- as.logical(row$fast_mode == 1L)

# Generate deterministic dataset split
if (suite == "synth") {
  T_sim <- if (fast_mode) 15.0 else NULL
  task_data <- generate_synthetic_task_data(
    system_id = as.character(row$system_id),
    N_train = as.integer(row$sample_size),
    noise_eta = as.numeric(row$noise_eta),
    noise_regime = as.character(row$noise_regime),
    seed = as.integer(row$seed),
    T_max = T_sim
  )
} else {
  task_data <- generate_real_task_data(
    dataset_id = as.character(row$dataset_id),
    diff_method = as.character(row$diff_method),
    fold_id = as.integer(row$fold_id),
    seed = as.integer(row$seed)
  )
}

# Execute method under wall-clock (elapsed) timeout guard (cpu = Inf avoids premature RUSAGE_CHILDREN kills in mclapply)
t_start <- proc.time()[3]
gc_before <- gc(reset = TRUE)

fit_res <- tryCatch(
  R.utils::withTimeout({
    if (method_id %in% c(
      "BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned", "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
      "BGLM_Oracle_Basis", "BGLM_Poly_Basis", "BGLM_Overcomplete"
    )) {
      run_fbms_method(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data, cores = cores, fast_mode = fast_mode)
    } else if (method_id %in% c(
      "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
      "UQ_SINDy_Oracle", "UQ_SINDy_Poly", "SINDy_PI_Rational"
    )) {
      run_sindy_baseline(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data, n_boot = if (fast_mode) 40L else 80L)
    } else if (method_id %in% c("SR_PySR", "SR_Operon")) {
      run_sr_baseline(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data)
    } else {
      stop("Unknown method_id: ", method_id)
    }
  }, timeout = timeout_sec, cpu = Inf, elapsed = timeout_sec, onTimeout = "error"),
  error = function(e) {
    warning("Method ", method_id, " encountered error on task ", task_id, ": ", e$message)
    NULL
  }
)

runtime_sec <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t_start)
gc_after <- gc()
peak_mem_mb <- sum(gc_after[, 6], na.rm = TRUE)

# Compute all structural, calibration, predictive, and trajectory rollout metrics
m <- ncol(task_data$Y_train)
if (is.null(fit_res)) {
  discovered_empty <- replicate(m, character(0), simplify = FALSE)
  st_metrics <- compute_structure_metrics(discovered_empty, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pip_brier <- NA_real_
  pred_tr <- list(R2_clipped = 0.0, R2_raw = 0.0, NRMSE = 1.0, log10_RMSE = 0.0)
  pred_in <- list(R2_clipped = 0.0, R2_raw = 0.0, NRMSE = 1.0, log10_RMSE = 0.0, Coverage_95 = NA_real_, Winkler_95 = NA_real_)
  pred_ood <- list(R2_clipped = 0.0, R2_raw = 0.0, NRMSE = 1.0, log10_RMSE = 0.0, Coverage_95 = NA_real_, Winkler_95 = NA_real_)
  rollout_m <- list(R2_traj = 0.0, NRMSE_traj = 1.0, T_valid = 0.0, T_valid_frac = 0.0)
  status_str <- "FAILED"
} else {
  st_metrics <- compute_structure_metrics(fit_res$discovered, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pip_brier <- compute_pip_brier_score(fit_res$pip_list, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pred_tr <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_train, task_data$Y_train_clean)
  pred_in <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_in, task_data$Y_test_in_noisy, fit_res$lower_in, fit_res$upper_in)
  pred_ood <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_ood, task_data$Y_test_ood_noisy, fit_res$lower_ood, fit_res$upper_ood)
  pred_in_clean <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_in, task_data$Y_test_in_clean)
  pred_ood_clean <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_ood, task_data$Y_test_ood_clean)
  pred_in$R2_clipped <- pred_in_clean$R2_clipped
  pred_in$R2_raw <- pred_in_clean$R2_raw
  pred_ood$R2_clipped <- pred_ood_clean$R2_clipped
  pred_ood$R2_raw <- pred_ood_clean$R2_raw
  rollout_m <- evaluate_trajectory_rollout(fit_res$predict_rhs_fn, task_data$rollout)
  status_str <- "OK"
}

if (suite == "synth") {
  out_dt <- data.table(
    task_id = task_id,
    suite = "synth",
    System = as.character(row$system_id),
    SystemGroup = as.character(row$system_group),
    SampleSize = as.integer(row$sample_size),
    Noise = as.numeric(row$noise_eta),
    NoiseRegime = as.character(row$noise_regime),
    Rep_ID = as.integer(row$rep_id),
    Seed = as.integer(row$seed),
    Method = method_id,
    Status = status_str,
    TPR = st_metrics$TPR,
    FDR = st_metrics$FDR,
    F1 = st_metrics$F1,
    Exact_Recovery = st_metrics$Exact_Recovery,
    SHD = st_metrics$SHD,
    OC = st_metrics$OC,
    n_active_terms = st_metrics$n_active_terms,
    PIP_Brier = pip_brier,
    R2_train = pred_tr$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_in = pred_in$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_ood = pred_ood$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_ood_raw = pred_ood$R2_raw,
    NRMSE_ood = pred_ood$NRMSE,
    log10_RMSE_ood = pred_ood$log10_RMSE,
    Coverage_95_in = pred_in$Coverage_95,
    Winkler_95_in = pred_in$Winkler_95,
    Coverage_95_ood = pred_ood$Coverage_95,
    Winkler_95_ood = pred_ood$Winkler_95,
    R2_traj = rollout_m$R2_traj,
    NRMSE_traj = rollout_m$NRMSE_traj,
    T_valid = rollout_m$T_valid,
    T_valid_frac = rollout_m$T_valid_frac,
    Runtime_s = runtime_sec,
    Peak_Mem_MB = peak_mem_mb,
    Discovered_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_hat, collapse = ";"),
    True_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_star, collapse = ";")
  )
} else {
  out_dt <- data.table(
    task_id = task_id,
    suite = "real",
    Dataset = as.character(row$dataset_id),
    Domain = as.character(row$domain),
    DiffMethod = as.character(row$diff_method),
    Fold_ID = as.integer(row$fold_id),
    Seed = as.integer(row$seed),
    Method = method_id,
    Status = status_str,
    TPR = st_metrics$TPR,
    FDR = st_metrics$FDR,
    F1 = st_metrics$F1,
    Exact_Recovery = st_metrics$Exact_Recovery,
    SHD = st_metrics$SHD,
    OC = st_metrics$OC,
    n_active_terms = st_metrics$n_active_terms,
    PIP_Brier = pip_brier,
    R2_train = pred_tr$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_in = pred_in$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_ood = pred_ood$R2_clipped,
    NRMSE_deriv_ood = pred_ood$NRMSE,
    Coverage_95_ood = pred_ood$Coverage_95,
    Winkler_95_ood = pred_ood$Winkler_95,
    R2_traj = rollout_m$R2_traj,
    NRMSE_traj = rollout_m$NRMSE_traj,
    T_valid = rollout_m$T_valid,
    T_valid_frac = rollout_m$T_valid_frac,
    Runtime_s = runtime_sec,
    Peak_Mem_MB = peak_mem_mb,
    Discovered_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_hat, collapse = ";"),
    Reference_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_star, collapse = ";")
  )
}

saveRDS(out_dt, ckpt_file)
message(sprintf("[DONE] suite=%s task_id=%d method=%s F1=%.3f R2_ood=%.3f R2_traj=%.3f (%.2fs)",
                suite, task_id, method_id, out_dt$F1, out_dt$R2_test_ood, out_dt$R2_traj, runtime_sec))


================================================================================
FILE: scripts/run_targeted_ablation.R
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_targeted_ablation.R
# Targeted Follow-Up Ablation:
#   1. Noise-regime comparison: derivative_snr vs. state_noise (errors-in-variables)
#   2. Native Julia/C++ Symbolic Regression (PYSR_USE_JULIA=1, PYOPERON_NATIVE=1)
#   3. Standardized-threshold UQ_SINDy fix (SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH=0)
#   4. Authentic 1900-1920 Hudson Bay Company Lynx-Hare pelt data
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
  library(R.utils)
})

source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
source("R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
source("R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

dir.create("results/ablation_native", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

# Ensure native SR, fixed standardized UQ-SINDy, and authentic Lynx-Hare are active
Sys.setenv(
  PYSR_USE_JULIA = Sys.getenv("PYSR_USE_JULIA", "1"),
  PYOPERON_NATIVE = Sys.getenv("PYOPERON_NATIVE", "1"),
  SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH = "0",
  USE_AUTHENTIC_EMPIRICAL = "1"
)

smoke_mode <- (Sys.getenv("ABLATION_SMOKE", "0") == "1")
cores_arg <- as.integer(Sys.getenv("CORES", "4"))
timeout_sec <- as.numeric(Sys.getenv("TASK_TIMEOUT", "600"))

seeds <- if (smoke_mode) c(101L) else c(101L, 202L, 303L)
systems <- if (smoke_mode) c("sys02_lorenz63") else c("sys02_lorenz63", "sys08_rossler_lorenz_hybrid", "sys11_goodwin_mm")
noise_regimes <- c("derivative_snr", "state_noise")
methods_synth <- c(
  "BGNLM_Full_Tuned",
  "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle",
  "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
  "UQ_SINDy_Poly",
  "SR_PySR",
  "SR_Operon"
)

run_method_dispatch <- function(method_id, task_data, cores = 4L, fast_mode = FALSE) {
  if (method_id %in% c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned", "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
    "BGLM_Oracle_Basis", "BGLM_Poly_Basis", "BGLM_Overcomplete"
  )) {
    run_fbms_method(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data, cores = cores, fast_mode = fast_mode)
  } else if (method_id %in% c(
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
    "UQ_SINDy_Oracle", "UQ_SINDy_Poly", "SINDy_PI_Rational"
  )) {
    run_sindy_baseline(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data, n_boot = if (fast_mode) 20L else 80L)
  } else if (method_id %in% c("SR_PySR", "SR_Operon")) {
    run_sr_baseline(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data)
  } else {
    stop("Unknown method_id: ", method_id)
  }
}

score_method_fit <- function(fit_res, task_data, method_id) {
  if (is.null(fit_res)) {
    return(list(
      Status = "FAILED", Engine = "failed", F1 = 0.0, TPR = 0.0, FDR = 0.0, Exact = 0L,
      SHD = NA_integer_, OC = 0L, PIP_Brier = NA_real_,
      R2_ood = 0.0, R2_traj = 0.0, Coverage_95_ood = NA_real_
    ))
  }
  eng <- if (!is.null(fit_res$engine)) as.character(fit_res$engine)[1L] else "r_native"
  st_m <- compute_structure_metrics(fit_res$discovered, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pip_b <- compute_pip_brier_score(fit_res$pip_list, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pred_ood_noisy <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_ood, task_data$Y_test_ood_noisy, fit_res$lower_ood, fit_res$upper_ood)
  pred_ood_clean <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_ood, task_data$Y_test_ood_clean)
  roll_m <- evaluate_trajectory_rollout(fit_res$predict_rhs_fn, task_data$rollout)
  list(
    Status = "OK",
    Engine = eng,
    F1 = st_m$F1,
    TPR = st_m$TPR,
    FDR = st_m$FDR,
    Exact = st_m$Exact_Recovery,
    SHD = st_m$SHD,
    OC = st_m$OC,
    PIP_Brier = pip_b,
    R2_ood = pred_ood_clean$R2_clipped,
    R2_traj = roll_m$R2_traj,
    Coverage_95_ood = pred_ood_noisy$Coverage_95
  )
}

build_authentic_lynx_hare_task <- function(csv_path, diff_method = "sgolay_spline", fold_id = 1L, seed = 1900L) {
  df <- read.csv(csv_path, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
  times <- as.numeric(df$time)
  state_names <- c("x1", "x2")
  X_states <- as.matrix(df[, state_names, drop = FALSE])
  Y_est <- if (diff_method == "sgolay_spline") {
    estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline(X_states, times)
  } else {
    estimate_derivatives_tvregdiff(X_states, times)
  }
  n_total <- nrow(X_states)
  set.seed(as.integer(seed))
  frac_train <- 0.65 + 0.10 * ((fold_id - 1L) %% 10L) / 9.0
  n_train_window <- max(20L, floor(frac_train * n_total))
  idx_train_pool <- seq_len(n_train_window)
  idx_ood <- seq(from = n_train_window + 1L, to = n_total)
  n_sub <- max(15L, floor(0.85 * length(idx_train_pool)))
  idx_train <- sort(sample(idx_train_pool, size = n_sub, replace = FALSE))
  idx_in <- setdiff(idx_train_pool, idx_train)
  if (length(idx_in) < 3L) idx_in <- idx_train
  rollout_idx <- idx_ood[seq_len(min(length(idx_ood), 100L))]
  rollout_times <- times[rollout_idx] - times[rollout_idx[1]]

  list(
    system = list(
      sys_id = 101L,
      id = "authentic_lynx_hare_1900_1920",
      name = "Authentic 1900-1920 Hudson Bay Lynx-Hare Pelts",
      group = "Ecology",
      dim = 2L,
      state_names = state_names,
      feature_names = state_names,
      input_names = character(0),
      ground_truth = list(c("x1", "x1*x2"), c("x2", "x1*x2")),
      oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
    ),
    dataset_id = "authentic_lynx_hare_1900_1920",
    diff_method = diff_method,
    fold_id = fold_id,
    seed = seed,
    N_train = length(idx_train),
    X_train = X_states[idx_train, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_train = Y_est[idx_train, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_train_clean = Y_est[idx_train, , drop = FALSE],
    X_test_in = X_states[idx_in, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_test_in_clean = Y_est[idx_in, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_test_in_noisy = Y_est[idx_in, , drop = FALSE],
    X_test_ood = X_states[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_test_ood_clean = Y_est[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE],
    Y_test_ood_noisy = Y_est[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE],
    rollout = list(
      times = rollout_times,
      X_true = X_states[rollout_idx, , drop = FALSE],
      X_inputs = NULL,
      x0 = X_states[rollout_idx[1], ]
    )
  )
}

# 1. Synthetic derivative_snr vs. state_noise ablation
synth_rows <- list()
total_synth <- length(systems) * length(noise_regimes) * length(seeds) * length(methods_synth)
idx_run <- 0L

for (sys_id in systems) {
  for (reg in noise_regimes) {
    for (sd in seeds) {
      task_data <- generate_synthetic_task_data(
        system_id = sys_id,
        N_train = if (smoke_mode) 80L else 250L,
        noise_eta = 0.05,
        noise_regime = reg,
        seed = sd,
        T_max = if (smoke_mode) 10.0 else NULL
      )
      for (m_id in methods_synth) {
        idx_run <- idx_run + 1L
        t0 <- proc.time()[3]
        fit_res <- tryCatch(
          R.utils::withTimeout(
            run_method_dispatch(m_id, task_data, cores = cores_arg, fast_mode = smoke_mode),
            timeout = timeout_sec, cpu = Inf, elapsed = timeout_sec, onTimeout = "error"
          ),
          error = function(e) {
            message("[WARN] ", sys_id, " | ", reg, " | ", m_id, " failed: ", e$message)
            NULL
          }
        )
        elapsed <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t0)
        ev <- score_method_fit(fit_res, task_data, m_id)
        message(sprintf(
          "[SYNTH %d/%d] %s | %s | seed=%d | %s (%s) -> %s (F1=%.3f, FDR=%.3f, R2_traj=%.3f, %.2fs)",
          idx_run, total_synth, sys_id, reg, sd, m_id, ev$Engine, ev$Status, ev$F1, ev$FDR, ev$R2_traj, elapsed
        ))
        synth_rows[[length(synth_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
          System = sys_id, NoiseRegime = reg, Seed = sd, Method = m_id, Engine = ev$Engine,
          Status = ev$Status, F1 = ev$F1, TPR = ev$TPR, FDR = ev$FDR, Exact = ev$Exact,
          OC = ev$OC, PIP_Brier = ev$PIP_Brier,
          R2_ood = ev$R2_ood, R2_traj = ev$R2_traj, Coverage_95_ood = ev$Coverage_95_ood,
          Runtime_s = elapsed
        )
      }
    }
  }
}

dt_synth_abl <- rbindlist(synth_rows, fill = TRUE)
out_synth_csv <- if (smoke_mode) "results/ablation_native/smoke_synth.csv" else "results/ablation_native/ablation_synth_results.csv"
fwrite(dt_synth_abl, out_synth_csv)
message("[OK] Saved ", out_synth_csv, " (", nrow(dt_synth_abl), " rows).")

# 2. Authentic 1900-1920 Hudson Bay Company Lynx-Hare empirical benchmark
source("scripts/download_real_data.R")
auth_csv <- "data/real_world/lynx_hare_authentic_1900_1920.csv"
if (file.exists(auth_csv)) {
  folds <- if (smoke_mode) 1L else 1:5
  real_rows <- list()
  for (fid in folds) {
    auth_task <- build_authentic_lynx_hare_task(auth_csv, diff_method = "sgolay_spline", fold_id = fid, seed = 1900L + fid)
    for (m_id in methods_synth) {
      t0 <- proc.time()[3]
      fit_res <- tryCatch(
        R.utils::withTimeout(
          run_method_dispatch(m_id, auth_task, cores = cores_arg, fast_mode = smoke_mode),
          timeout = timeout_sec, cpu = Inf, elapsed = timeout_sec, onTimeout = "error"
        ),
        error = function(e) {
          message("[WARN] authentic_lynx_hare | fold=", fid, " | ", m_id, " failed: ", e$message)
          NULL
        }
      )
      elapsed <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t0)
      ev <- score_method_fit(fit_res, auth_task, m_id)
      message(sprintf(
        "[AUTH REAL fold=%d] %s (%s) -> %s (F1=%.3f, FDR=%.3f, R2_traj=%.3f, %.2fs)",
        fid, m_id, ev$Engine, ev$Status, ev$F1, ev$FDR, ev$R2_traj, elapsed
      ))
      real_rows[[length(real_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
        Dataset = "authentic_lynx_hare_1900_1920", Fold_ID = fid, Method = m_id, Engine = ev$Engine,
        Status = ev$Status, F1 = ev$F1, TPR = ev$TPR, FDR = ev$FDR, Exact = ev$Exact,
        OC = ev$OC, R2_ood = ev$R2_ood, R2_traj = ev$R2_traj, Coverage_95_ood = ev$Coverage_95_ood,
        Runtime_s = elapsed
      )
    }
  }
  dt_real_abl <- rbindlist(real_rows, fill = TRUE)
  out_real_csv <- if (smoke_mode) "results/ablation_native/smoke_real.csv" else "results/ablation_native/ablation_authentic_lynx_hare.csv"
  fwrite(dt_real_abl, out_real_csv)
  message("[OK] Saved ", out_real_csv, " (", nrow(dt_real_abl), " rows).")
}

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/setup_server.sh
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# ==============================================================================
# scripts/setup_server.sh
# One-shot bootstrap for remote Linux servers (e.g., nam-shub-01):
# Creates self-contained project-local R (.r_lib/) and Python (.venv/) environments
# ==============================================================================

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

echo "[SETUP] Repository root: $REPO_ROOT"

# Load R module on EasyBuild/Lmod clusters if Rscript isn't already available or module exists
if command -v module >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
elif [ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if ! command -v Rscript >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERROR] Rscript not found! Please run 'module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b' (or equivalent) first."
  exit 1
fi

echo "[SETUP] Using Rscript: $(command -v Rscript)"
mkdir -p "$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib" results/checkpoints_synth results/checkpoints_real logs

export R_LIBS_USER="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
export R_LIBS="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
export R_MAKEVARS_USER=/dev/null

# Ensure project-local Python .venv uses Python >= 3.9 (RHEL8 default python3 is 3.6 + pip 9.0.3)
venv_needs_rebuild=0
if [ ! -x "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" ]; then
  venv_needs_rebuild=1
else
  py_ok=$("$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" -c 'import sys; print(int(sys.version_info >= (3, 9)))' 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
  if [ "$py_ok" != "1" ]; then
    echo "[SETUP] Existing .venv uses Python < 3.9; rebuilding with Python 3.11..."
    rm -rf "$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
    venv_needs_rebuild=1
  fi
fi

if [ "$venv_needs_rebuild" = "1" ]; then
  export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
  if ! command -v uv >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[SETUP] Installing uv locally to bootstrap standalone Python 3.11..."
    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh
    export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
  fi
  if command -v uv >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    uv venv "$REPO_ROOT/.venv" --python 3.11
    uv pip install --python "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" numpy scipy scikit-learn sympy pysindy pandas pyyaml
    uv pip install --python "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" pysr pyoperon || true
  else
    echo "[WARN] uv unavailable; falling back to module load Python..."
    module load Python 2>/dev/null || true
    python3 -m venv "$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
    "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/pip" install --upgrade pip
    "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/pip" install numpy scipy scikit-learn sympy pysindy pandas pyyaml
    "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/pip" install pysr pyoperon || true
  fi
fi

# Activate .venv so install_dependencies.R sees it
if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate" ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate"
fi

echo "[SETUP] Installing R packages into $REPO_ROOT/.r_lib and verifying .venv..."
Rscript scripts/install_dependencies.R

echo "[SETUP] Running pre-flight environment verification..."
Rscript scripts/verify_env.R

echo "[SETUP] Preparing real-world datasets..."
Rscript scripts/download_real_data.R

echo "[SETUP] Generating manifests if missing..."
if [[ ! -f "config/manifest_synth.csv" || ! -f "config/manifest_real.csv" ]]; then
  Rscript scripts/generate_manifest.R
fi

echo "[SETUP] Running unit test suite..."
Rscript -e 'testthat::test_dir("tests/testthat")'

echo "[OK] Server setup complete! You can now launch benchmarks with:"
echo "     nohup ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh both 40 1 > run_benchmarks.log 2>&1 &"


================================================================================
FILE: scripts/verify_env.R
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/verify_env.R
# Fast Pre-Flight Verification of Project-Local R (.r_lib) & Python (.venv)
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

message("[VERIFY] Active R version: ", R.version.string)
message("[VERIFY] Active .libPaths():\n  - ", paste(.libPaths(), collapse = "\n  - "))

required_r_pkgs <- c(
  "FBMS", "deSolve", "glmnet", "signal", "data.table", "yaml", "jsonlite",
  "lme4", "lmerTest", "glmmTMB", "emmeans", "broom.mixed",
  "ggplot2", "dplyr", "tidyr", "reticulate", "R.utils", "testthat"
)

for (pkg in required_r_pkgs) {
  ok <- tryCatch({
    suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(pkg, character.only = TRUE))
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    message("[FAIL] Cannot load R package '", pkg, "': ", e$message)
    FALSE
  })
  if (!ok) stop("Pre-flight R check failed on package: ", pkg)
}

# Verify Python .venv bridge
source("R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
py_bin <- find_python_bin()
message("[VERIFY] Python interpreter: ", py_bin)

py_chk <- system2(
  py_bin,
  c("-c", shQuote("import numpy, scipy, sklearn, sympy, pysindy, pandas, yaml; print('PYTHON_ENV_OK')")),
  stdout = TRUE, stderr = TRUE
)
if (!any(grepl("PYTHON_ENV_OK", py_chk, fixed = TRUE))) {
  stop("Pre-flight Python check failed! Output:\n", paste(py_chk, collapse = "\n"))
}

# Quick sanity check on FBMS + symbolic parser
source("R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
t_dicts <- register_fbms_transforms()
deep_test <- extract_canonical_atoms("p3(1+1*x1+1*x2+1*p3(1+1*x1+1*x2+1*p2(x1)))")
stopifnot(length(deep_test) >= 1L)

message("[OK] Pre-flight verification PASSED (R + FBMS ", as.character(packageVersion("FBMS")), " + Python .venv ready).")

FILE: slurm/submit_benchmark_array.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash
#SBATCH --job-name=icml_bgnlm_synth
#SBATCH --output=logs/slurm_synth_%A_%a.out
#SBATCH --error=logs/slurm_synth_%A_%a.err
#SBATCH --array=1-1000%100
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=10
#SBATCH --mem=16G
#SBATCH --time=04:00:00

set -euo pipefail

export OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
export OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1

SUITE=${SUITE:-synth}
CHUNK_SIZE=${CHUNK_SIZE:-22}
START_ID=$(( (SLURM_ARRAY_TASK_ID - 1) * CHUNK_SIZE + 1 ))
END_ID=$(( SLURM_ARRAY_TASK_ID * CHUNK_SIZE ))

TOTAL_TASKS=$(tail -n +2 "config/manifest_${SUITE}.csv" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
if [[ "$END_ID" -gt "$TOTAL_TASKS" ]]; then
  END_ID="$TOTAL_TASKS"
fi

for TASK_ID in $(seq "$START_ID" "$END_ID"); do
    Rscript scripts/run_single_task.R --suite "$SUITE" --task_id "$TASK_ID" --cores "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"
done

FILE: tests/testthat/test_methods_and_mixed_models.R

library(testthat)
source("../../R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("../../R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("../../R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
source("../../R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
source("../../R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
source("../../R/08_mixed_effects_eval.R")

test_that("FBMS, SINDy, and Symbolic Regression wrappers return standardized output objects", {
  td <- generate_synthetic_task_data(
    system_id = "sys04_lotka_volterra",
    N_train = 150,
    noise_eta = 0.01,
    seed = 42,
    T_max = 10.0
  )

  res_bgnlm <- run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td, cores = 1L, fast_mode = TRUE)
  expect_equal(length(res_bgnlm$discovered), 2L)
  expect_true(is.function(res_bgnlm$predict_rhs_fn))
  st_bgnlm <- compute_structure_metrics(res_bgnlm$discovered, td$system$ground_truth)
  expect_gte(st_bgnlm$TPR, 0.5)

  res_sindy <- run_sindy_baseline("SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", td)
  expect_equal(length(res_sindy$discovered), 2L)
  st_sindy <- compute_structure_metrics(res_sindy$discovered, td$system$ground_truth)
  expect_equal(st_sindy$TPR, 1.0)

  res_sr <- run_sr_baseline("SR_PySR", td)
  expect_equal(length(res_sr$discovered), 2L)
  expect_equal(dim(res_sr$pred_ood), dim(td$Y_test_ood_clean))
})


================================================================================
FILE: tests/testthat/test_simulators.R
================================================================================
library(testthat)
source("../../R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("../../R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

test_that("All 12 synthetic ODE systems integrate cleanly and match analytical derivatives with R2 > 0.9999", {
  reg <- get_systems_registry()
  expect_equal(length(reg), 12L)

  for (sys_id in names(reg)) {
    traj <- simulate_base_trajectory(sys_id, T_max = 5.0, use_cache = FALSE)
    expect_true(all(is.finite(traj$X)), info = paste("Non-finite states in", sys_id))
    expect_true(all(is.finite(traj$dX_analytical)), info = paste("Non-finite analytical deriv in", sys_id))

    # Verify finite difference vs analytical derivative has R2 > 0.9999 at dt = 1e-4
    for (j in seq_len(traj$system$dim)) {
      r2_fd <- cor(traj$dX_fd[, j], traj$dX_analytical[, j])^2
      expect_gt(r2_fd, 0.9999, label = paste(sys_id, "eq", j, "FD vs analytical R2"))

      # Verify ground-truth basis atoms in Oracle library yield R2 > 0.9999 against analytical derivative
      Phi_oracle <- build_oracle_library(traj$X[1:1000, , drop = FALSE], extra_atoms = traj$system$oracle_extra_basis)
      gt_atoms <- traj$system$ground_truth[[j]]
      expect_true(all(gt_atoms %in% colnames(Phi_oracle)),
                  info = paste(sys_id, "eq", j, "missing atoms in oracle:", paste(setdiff(gt_atoms, colnames(Phi_oracle)), collapse = ",")))
      fit_lm <- lm(traj$dX_analytical[1:1000, j] ~ Phi_oracle[, gt_atoms, drop = FALSE])
      r2_gt <- suppressWarnings(summary(fit_lm)$r.squared)
      expect_gt(r2_gt, 0.9999, label = paste(sys_id, "eq", j, "ground-truth basis R2"))
    }
  }
})

test_that("Overcomplete library has > 60 candidate columns and finite values", {
  traj <- simulate_base_trajectory("sys02_lorenz63", T_max = 2.0, use_cache = FALSE)
  Phi_over <- build_overcomplete_library(traj$X[1:200, , drop = FALSE])
  expect_gt(ncol(Phi_over), 60L)
  expect_true(all(is.finite(Phi_over)))
})

================================================================================
FILE: tests/testthat/test_symbolic_and_metrics.R
================================================================================
library(testthat)
source("../../R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("../../R/06_metrics_consistency.R")

test_that("Algebraically equivalent strings across FBMS, PySR, PyOperon, and PySINDy canonicalize identically", {
  # Commutative products
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x2 * x1"), "x1*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("(x.2 * x.1)"), "x1*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x1 x2"), "x1*x2")

  # Powers and FBMS transforms
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("p2(x1) * x2"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x2 * (x1^2)"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("square(x_1) * x_2"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x1**2 * x2"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("p3(x.1)"), "x1^3")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("cube(x1)"), "x1^3")

  # Trigonometric and fractional transforms
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("sin_rad(x1)"), "sin(x1)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("sin_deg(x.1)"), "sin(x1)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("p05(x.1)"), "sqrt(x1)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("sqroot(x1)"), "sqrt(x1)")

  # Rational / Hill transforms
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("inv_sq(x.3)"), "1/(1+x3^2)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x.3 * inv_pos(x.3)"), "x3/(1+x3)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x.1 * hill_2(x.2)"), "x1*x2^2/(1+x2^2)")

  # Full expanded equation from PySR / PyOperon
  expect_equal(
    extract_canonical_atoms("x1 * (28.0 - x3) - 1.0 * x2"),
    c("x1", "x1*x3", "x2")
  )
})

test_that("Selection consistency metrics produce TPR=1, FDR=0, Exact_Recovery=1 for equivalent representations", {
  gt_lorenz <- list(
    c("x1", "x2"),
    c("x1", "x2", "x1*x3"),
    c("x3", "x1*x2")
  )
  discovered_fbms <- list(
    c("x.2", "x.1"),
    c("(x.3 * x.1)", "x.1", "x.2"),
    c("(x.2*x.1)", "x.3")
  )
  m <- compute_structure_metrics(discovered_fbms, gt_lorenz)
  expect_equal(m$TPR, 1.0)
  expect_equal(m$FDR, 0.0)
  expect_equal(m$F1, 1.0)
  expect_equal(m$Exact_Recovery, 1L)
  expect_equal(m$SHD, 0L)
})

test_that("Jaccard and Nogueira stability estimators work properly", {
  sets_perfect <- replicate(10, c("eq1::x1", "eq1::x2", "eq2::x1*x3"), simplify = FALSE)
  expect_equal(compute_jaccard_stability(sets_perfect), 1.0)
  nog <- compute_nogueira_stability(sets_perfect, d_total = 20)
  expect_equal(nog$phi, 1.0)
})
